}\n      }\n    } catch {\n      // 読み込みエラー時は初期データを使用\n    }\n    return initialCards;\n  });\n\n  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useMemo, useEffect, useCallback, ChangeEvent, FormEvent } from 'react';\nimport { BookOpen, Sparkles, Plus, AlertCircle, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { FlashCard, FilterCategory, CardStatusFilter, CardFormData } from './types';\nimport { initialCards } from './sampleData';\nimport FlashCardItem from './components/FlashCardItem';\nimport CardControls from './components/CardControls';\n\nconst initialFormState: CardFormData = {\n  word: '',\n  phonetic: '',\n  partOfSpeech: '名詞',\n  meaning: '',\n  exampleEn: '',\n  exampleJa: '',\n  category: 'daily',\n};\n\nconst generateId = (): string => {\n  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') {\n    return crypto.randomUUID();\n  }\n  return `card-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9)}`;\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [cards, setCards] = useState<FlashCard[]>(() => {\n    try {\n      const saved = localStorage.getItem('flashcards_data');\n      if (saved) {\n        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);\n        if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) {\n          return parsed;\n        }\n      }\n    } catch {\n      // 読み込みエラー時は初期データを使用\n    }\n    return initialCards;\n  });\n\n  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<FilterCategory>('all');\n  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<CardStatusFilter>('all');\n  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // 新規単語登録用フォームステート\n  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<CardFormData>(initialFormState);\n\n  // ローカルストレージ自動保存\n  useEffect(() => {\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem('flashcards_data', JSON.stringify(cards));\n    } catch {\n      // 保存不可時のフォールバック\n    }\n  }, [cards]);\n\n  // フィルタリング処理\n  const filteredCards = useMemo(() => {\n    return cards.filter((card) => {\n      const categoryMatch = selectedCategory === 'all' || card.category === selectedCategory;\n      const statusMatch =\n        statusFilter === 'all' ||\n        (statusFilter === 'mastered' && card.isMastered) ||\n        (statusFilter === 'learning' && !card.isMastered);\n      return categoryMatch && statusMatch;\n    });\n  }, [cards, selectedCategory, statusFilter]);\n\n  // フィルタ切り替え時にインデックス範囲を安全に補正\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  }, [selectedCategory, statusFilter]);\n\n  // フィルタ後の要素数が変化した際の安全なインデックスガード\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (filteredCards.length > 0 && currentIndex >= filteredCards.length) {\n      setCurrentIndex(filteredCards.length - 1);\n    }\n  }, [filteredCards.length, currentIndex]);\n\n  // 統計情報計算\n  const totalCount = cards.length;\n  const masteredCount = useMemo(() => cards.filter((c) => c.isMastered).length, [cards]);\n  const learningCount = totalCount - masteredCount;\n  const masterPercentage = totalCount > 0 ? Math.round((masteredCount / totalCount) * 100) : 0;\n\n  // 現在のカード\n  const currentCard = filteredCards[currentIndex];\n\n  const handleFlip = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsFlipped((prev) => !prev);\n  }, []);\n\n  const handlePrev = useCallback(() => {\n    if (currentIndex > 0) {\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n      setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev - 1);\n    }\n  }, [currentIndex]);\n\n  const handleNext = useCallback(() => {\n    if (currentIndex < filteredCards.length - 1) {\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n      setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev + 1);\n    }\n  }, [currentIndex, filteredCards.length]);\n\n  const updateCardMasteredStatus = useCallback(\n    (isMastered: boolean) => {\n      if (!currentCard) return;\n\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n\n      const willBeRemovedFromFilter =\n        (statusFilter === 'learning' && isMastered) ||\n        (statusFilter === 'mastered' && !isMastered);\n\n      if (willBeRemovedFromFilter) {\n        if (currentIndex >= filteredCards.length - 1) {\n          setCurrentIndex((prev) => Math.max(0, prev - 1));\n        }\n      } else {\n        if (currentIndex < filteredCards.length - 1) {\n          setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev + 1);\n        }\n      }\n\n      setCards((prev) =>\n        prev.map((c) => (c.id === currentCard.id ? { ...c, isMastered } : c))\n      );\n    },\n    [currentCard, currentIndex, filteredCards.length, statusFilter]\n  );\n\n  const handleMarkMastered = useCallback(() => {\n    updateCardMasteredStatus(true);\n  }, [updateCardMasteredStatus]);\n\n  const handleMarkLearning = useCallback(() => {\n    updateCardMasteredStatus(false);\n  }, [updateCardMasteredStatus]);\n\n  const handleShuffle = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setCards((prev) => {\n      const copy = [...prev];\n      for (let i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n        [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]];\n      }\n      return copy;\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleResetFilters = () => {\n    setSelectedCategory('all');\n    setStatusFilter('all');\n  };\n\n  // キーボードショートカット (左右キーで移動、Spaceキーで反転)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (isModalOpen) return;\n\n      if (e.code === 'Space') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleFlip();\n      } else if (e.code === 'ArrowLeft') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handlePrev();\n      } else if (e.code === 'ArrowRight') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleNext();\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, [handleFlip, handlePrev, handleNext, isModalOpen]);\n\n  const handleFormChange = (\n    e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>\n  ) => {\n    const { name, value } = e.target;\n    setFormData((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      [name]: value,\n    }));\n  };\n\n  // 新規単語追加送信\n  const handleAddCardSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    const trimmedWord = formData.word.trim();\n    const trimmedMeaning = formData.meaning.trim();\n    if (!trimmedWord || !trimmedMeaning) return;\n\n    const newCardItem: FlashCard = {\n      id: generateId(),\n      word: trimmedWord,\n      phonetic: formData.phonetic.trim() || `/${trimmedWord.toLowerCase()}/`,\n      partOfSpeech: formData.partOfSpeech,\n      meaning: trimmedMeaning,\n      exampleEn: formData.exampleEn.trim() || `I practice using \"${trimmedWord}\" every day.`,\n      exampleJa: formData.exampleJa.trim() || `私は「${trimmedWord}」を毎日使う練習をしています。`,\n      category: formData.category,\n      isMastered: false,\n    };\n\n    setCards((prev) => [newCardItem, ...prev]);\n    setIsModalOpen(false);\n    setSelectedCategory('all');\n    setStatusFilter('all');\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setFormData(initialFormState);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div  </h1>\n        <p className=\"header-subtitle\">\n          立体的なカード反転と音声発音で楽しくスピーディーに語彙力を定着\n        </p>\n      </header>\n\n      {/* 統計バー & 進捗状況 */}\n      <section>\n        <div </div>\n          <div className=\"stat-item\">\n            <span className=\"stat-value\" style={{ color: '#10b981' }}>\n              {masteredCount}\n            </span>\n            <span {learningCount}\n            </span>\n            <span className=\"stat-label\">学習中</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"stat-item\">\n            <span className=\"stat-value\"           role=\"progressbar\"\n              aria-valuenow={masterPercentage}\n              aria-valuemin={0}\n              aria-valuemax={100}\n            />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </section>\n\n      {/* フィルターセクション */}\n      <section className=\"filter-section\">\n        <div className=\"category-tabs\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n 'tab-btn tab-btn-active' : 'tab-btn'}\n            onClick={() => setSelectedCategory('daily')}\n          >\n            日常会話\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={selectedCategory === 'business' ? 'tab-btn tab-btn-active' : 'tab-btn'}\n            onClick={() => setSelectedCategory('business')}\n          >\n            ビジネス\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={selectedCategory === 'academic' ? 'tab-btn tab-btn-active' : 'tab-btn'}\n            onClick={() => setSelectedCategory('academic')}\n          >\n            学術・教養\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={selectedCategory === 'travel' ? 'tab-btn tab-btn-active' : 'tab-btn'}\n            onClick={() => setSelectedCategory('travel')}\n          >\n            旅行\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"status-filters\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={statusFilter === 'all' ? 'status-btn status-btn-active' : 'status-btn'}\n            onClick={() => setStatusFilter('all')}\n          >\n            全ステータス\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={statusFilter === 'learning' ? 'status-btn status-btn-active' : 'status-btn'}\n            onClick={() => setStatusFilter('learning')}\n          >\n            学習中のみ\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={statusFilter === 'mastered' ? 'status-btn status-btn-active' : 'status-btn'}\n            onClick={() => setStatusFilter('mastered')}\n          >\n            習得済みのみ\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </section>\n\n      {/* カード表示領域 */}\n      <main>\n        {filteredCards.length > 0 && currentCard ? (\n          <>\n            <FlashCardItem\n              card={currentCard}\n              isFlipped={isFlipped}\n              onFlip={handleFlip}\n              currentIndex={currentIndex}\n              totalCards={filteredCards.length}\n            />\n\n            <CardControls\n              onPrev={handlePrev}\n              onNext={handleNext}\n              onMarkMastered={handleMarkMastered}\n              onMarkLearning={handleMarkLearning}\n              onShuffle={handleShuffle}\n              onOpenAddModal={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}\n              canPrev={currentIndex > 0}\n              canNext={currentIndex < filteredCards.length - 1}\n              isMastered={currentCard.isMastered}\n            />\n          </>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"empty-state\">\n            <AlertCircle size={40} color=\"#94a3b8\" />\n            <p>条件に一致する英単語カードが見つかりません。</p>\n            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>\n categoryMatch = selectedCategory === 'all' || card.category === selectedCategory;\n      const statusMatch =\n        statusFilter === 'all' ||\n        (statusFilter === 'mastered' && card.isMastered) ||\n        (statusFilter === 'learning' && !card.isMastered);\n      return categoryMatch && statusMatch;\n    });\n  }, [cards, selectedCategory, statusFilter]);\n\n  // フィルタ切り替え時にインデックス範囲を安全に補正\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  }, [selectedCategory, statusFilter]);\n\n  // フィルタ後の要素数が変化した際の安全なインデックスガード\n  useEffect(() => {\n totalCount > 0 ? Math.round((masteredCount / totalCount) * 100) : 0;\n\n  // 現在のカード\n  const currentCard = filteredCards[currentIndex];\n\n  const handleFlip = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsFlipped((prev) => !prev);\n };\n\n  // キーボードショートカット (左右キーで移動、Spaceキーで反転)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (isModalOpen) return;\n\n      if (e.code === 'Space') {\n utterance.onend = () => {\n        setIsSpeaking(false);\n      };\n\n      utterance.onerror = () => {\n        setIsSpeaking(false);\n        setSpeechError('音声の再生中にエラーが発生しました。');\n      };\n\n 'daily',\n};\n\nconst generateId = (): string => {\n  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') {\n    return crypto.randomUUID();\n  }\n  return `card-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9)}`;\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [cards, setCards] = useState<FlashCard[]>(() => {\n    try {\n      const saved = }\n      }\n    } catch {\n      // 読み込みエラー時は初期データを使用\n    }\n    return initialCards;\n  });\n\n  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<FilterCategory>('all');\n  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<CardStatusFilter>('all');\n  const => {\n    return cards.filter((card) => {\n      const categoryMatch = selectedCategory === 'all' || card.category === selectedCategory;\n      const statusMatch =\n        statusFilter === 'all' ||\n        (statusFilter === 'mastered' && card.isMastered) ||\n        (statusFilter === 'learning' && !card.isMastered);\n      return categoryMatch && statusMatch;\n    });\n  }, [cards, selectedCategory, statusFilter]);\n\n  // フィルタ切り替え時にインデックス範囲を安全に補正\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  }, [selectedCategory, statusFilter]);\n\n  // フィルタ後の要素数が変化した際の安全なインデックスガード\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (filteredCards.length > 0 && currentIndex >= filteredCards.length) {\n      setCurrentIndex(filteredCards.length - 1);\n    }\n  }, [filteredCards.length, currentIndex]);\n\n  // 統計情報計算\n  const totalCount = cards.length;\n  const masteredCount = useMemo(() => cards.filter((c) => c.isMastered).length, [cards]);\n  const Math.round((masteredCount / totalCount) * 100) : 0;\n\n  // 現在のカード\n  const currentCard = filteredCards[currentIndex];\n\n  const handleFlip = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsFlipped((prev) => !prev);\n  }, []);\n\n  const handlePrev = useCallback(() => {\n    if (currentIndex > 0) {\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n      setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev - 1);\n    }\n  }, [currentIndex]);\n\n  const filteredCards.length]);\n\n  const updateCardMasteredStatus = useCallback(\n    (isMastered: boolean) => {\n      if (!currentCard) return;\n\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n\n      const willBeRemovedFromFilter =\n        (statusFilter === 'learning' && isMastered) ||\n        (statusFilter === 'mastered' && !isMastered);\n\n      if (willBeRemovedFromFilter) {\n        if (currentIndex >= filteredCards.length - 1) {\n          setCurrentIndex((prev) => Math.max(0, prev - 1));\n        }\n      } else {\n        if (currentIndex < filteredCards.length - 1) {\n          setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev + 1);\n        }\n      }\n\n      setCards((prev) =>\n        prev.map((c) => (c.id === currentCard.id ? { ...c, isMastered } : c))\n      );\n    },\n    [currentCard, currentIndex, filteredCards.length, statusFilter]\n  );\n\n  const handleMarkMastered = useCallback(() => {\n    updateCardMasteredStatus(true);\n  }, [updateCardMasteredStatus]);\n\n  const handleMarkLearning = useCallback(() => {\n    updateCardMasteredStatus(false);\n  }, [updateCardMasteredStatus]);\n\n  const handleShuffle = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setCards((prev) => {\n      const copy = [...prev];\n      for (let i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n        [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]];\n      }\n      return copy;\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleResetFilters = () => {\n    setSelectedCategory('all');\n    setStatusFilter('all');\n  };\n\n  // キーボードショートカット (左右キーで移動、Spaceキーで反転)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (isModalOpen) return;\n\n      if (e.code === 'Space') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleFlip();\n      } else if (e.code === 'ArrowLeft') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handlePrev();\n      } else if (e.code === 'ArrowRight') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleNext();\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, [handleFlip, handlePrev, handleNext, isModalOpen]);\n\n  const handleFormChange = (\n    e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>\n  ) => {\n    const { e.preventDefault();\n    const trimmedWord = formData.word.trim();\n    const trimmedMeaning = formData.meaning.trim();\n    if (!trimmedWord || !trimmedMeaning) return;\n\n    const newCardItem: setSelectedCategory('all');\n    setStatusFilter('all');\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setFormData(initialFormState);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      {/* アプリヘッダー */}\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <h1 className=\"header-title\">\n          <Sparkles size={28} />\n          英単語3Dフラッシュカード\n        </h1>\n        <p className=\"header-subtitle\">\n          立体的なカード反転と音声発音で楽しくスピーディーに語彙力を定着\n        </p>\n      </header>\n\n      {/* 統計バー & 進捗状況 */}\n      <section>\n        <div className=\"stats-bar\">\n          <div className=\"stat-item\">\n            <span className=\"stat-value\">{totalCount}</span>\n            <span className=\"stat-label\">総単語数</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"stat-item\">\n            <span className=\"stat-value stat-value-mastered\">\n              {masteredCount}\n            </span>\n            <span     </span>\n            <span className=\"stat-label\">学習中</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"stat-item\">\n            <span className=\"stat-value stat-value-total\">\n              {masterPercentage}%\n            </span>\n            <span className=\"stat-label\">達成率</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"progress-container\">\n          <div        totalCards={filteredCards.length}\n            />\n\n            <CardControls\n              onPrev={handlePrev}\n              onNext={handleNext}\n              onMarkMastered={handleMarkMastered}\n              onMarkLearning={handleMarkLearning}\n              onShuffle={handleShuffle}\n              onOpenAddModal={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}\n              canPrev={currentIndex > 0}\n              canNext={currentIndex < filteredCards.length - 1}\n              isMastered={currentCard.isMastered}\n            />\n          </>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"empty-state\">\n            <AlertCircle size={40} color=\"#94a3b8\" />\n            <p>条件に一致する英単語カードが見つかりません。</p>\n            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>\n              <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn-reset\" onClick={handleResetFilters}>\n                <RefreshCw size={14} style={{ display: 'inline', marginRight: '4px' }} />\n                フィルターを解除\n              </button>\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className=\"add-card-toggle\"\n                onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}\n              >\n                <Plus size={14} style={{ display: 'inline', marginRight: '4px' }} />\n                この条件で単語を追加\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </main>\n\n      {/* 単語追加モーダル */}\n      {isModalOpen && (\n        <div\n          className=\"modal-backdrop\"\n          onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}\n          role=\"dialog\"\n          aria-modal=\"true\"\n        >\n          <div className=\"modal-content\" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>\n            <h2 className=\"modal-title\">\n              <BookOpen size={22} className=\"form-input\"\n                  placeholder=\"例: persistent\"\n                  value={formData.word}\n                  onChange={handleFormChange}\n                  required\n                  autoFocus\n                />\n              </div>\n\n              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '12px' }}>\n                <div className=\"form-group\">\n                  <label htmlFor=\"card-phonetic-input\">発音記号</label>\n                  <input\n                    id=\"card-phonetic-input\"\n
